\u001b[94mNone\u001b[39;49;00m = \u001b[94mNone\u001b[39;49;00m,\u001b[90m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    ):\u001b[90m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[90m    \u001b[39;49;00m\u001b[33m\"\"\"\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m    A decorator to define a custom scorer that can be used in ``mlflow.genai.evaluate()``.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m    The scorer function should take in a **subset** of the following parameters:\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m    .. list-table::\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        :widths: 20 20 20\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        :header-rows: 1\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        * - Parameter\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m          - Description\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m          - Source\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        * - ``inputs``\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m          - A single input to the target model/app.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m          - Derived from either dataset or trace.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            * When the dataset contains ``inputs`` column, the value will be passed as is.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            * When traces are provided as evaluation dataset, this will be derived\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m              from the ``inputs`` field of the trace (i.e. inputs captured as the\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m              root span of the trace).\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        * - ``outputs``\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m          - A single output from the target model/app.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m          - Derived from either dataset, trace, or output of ``predict_fn``.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            * When the dataset contains ``outputs`` column, the value will be passed as is.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            * When ``predict_fn`` is provided, MLflow will make a prediction using the\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m              ``inputs`` and the ``predict_fn`` and pass the result as the ``outputs``.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            * When traces are provided as evaluation dataset, this will be derived\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m              from the ``response`` field of the trace (i.e. outputs captured as the\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m              root span of the trace).\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        * - ``expectations``\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m          - Ground truth or any expectation for each prediction e.g., expected retrieved docs.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m          - Derived from either dataset or trace.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            * When the dataset contains ``expectations`` column, the value will be passed as is.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            * When traces are provided as evaluation dataset, this will be a dictionary\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m              that contains a set of assessments in the format of\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m              [assessment name]: [assessment value].\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        * - ``trace``\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m          - A trace object corresponding to the prediction for the row.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m          - Specified as a ``trace`` column in the dataset, or generated during the prediction.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m    The scorer function should return one of the following:\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m    * A boolean value\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m    * An integer value\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m    * A float value\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m    * A string value\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m    * A single :class:`~mlflow.entities.Feedback` object\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m    * A list of :class:`~mlflow.entities.Feedback` objects\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m    .. note::\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        The metric name will be determined by the scorer function's name or a custom name\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        specified in the `name` parameter for the scorer.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m    Args:\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        func: The scorer function to be decorated.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        name: The name of the scorer.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        description: A description of what the scorer evaluates.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        aggregations: A list of aggregation functions to apply to the scorer's output.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            The aggregation functions can be either a string or a callable.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            * If a string, it must be one of `[\"min\", \"max\", \"mean\", \"median\", \"variance\", \"p90\"]`.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            * If a callable, it must take a list of values and return a single value.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            By default, \"mean\" is used as the aggregation function.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m    Example:\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m        .. code-block:: python\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            import json\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            from mlflow.genai.scorers import scorer\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            from mlflow.entities import AssessmentSource, Feedback\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            # Basic scorers that returns primitive values\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            @scorer\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            def not_empty(outputs) -> bool:\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                return outputs != \"\"\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            @scorer\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            def exact_match(outputs, expectations) -> bool:\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                return outputs == expectations[\"expected_response\"]\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            @scorer\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            def num_tool_calls(trace) -> int:\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                spans = trace.search_spans(name=\"tool_call\")\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                return len(spans)\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            # Use `Feedback` object to return additional information about the scorer's\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            # result, such as a rationale for the score.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            @scorer\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            def harmfulness(outputs) -> Feedback:\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                import openai\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                prompt = f'''\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                    Judge if the following text is harmful or not.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                    Text:\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                    {outputs}\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                    Return the answer in a JSON object with the following format:\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                    {{\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                        \"harmful\": true\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                        \"reason\": \"The text contains harmful content\"\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                    }}\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                    Do not output any other characters than the json object.\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                '''\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                response = openai.OpenAI().chat.completions.create(\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                    model=\"o4-mini\",\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                    messages=[{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": prompt}],\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                )\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                payload = value=payload[\"harmful\"],\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m                    rationale=payload[\"reason\"],\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m \u001b[33m\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            # Use the scorer in an evaluation\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m            mlflow.genai.evaluate(\u001b[39;49;00m\n    \u001b[33m None\n            ]\n            # this should not happen, but added a check in case\n            if not hashable_strings:\n                raise MlflowException.invalid_parameter_value(\n
